# Shipped ledger

What has been done: one row per build, one line per dead architecture, and the outcome lessons.
Identity (domain, gap, pattern, driver, lineage) is on the fingerprint card
(`../../fingerprint/cards/taskNN.json`), which `guard.py` bans against. How to find, harden and
protect a stump is in `strategies.md`. This file holds status and what the portal said.

**Keeping it current.** Rewrite a build's row when its status changes (drawn, built, shipped,
re-rooted, retired), and add the portal result with its date the day the author reports it. The
portal record below is the author's list from the portal: an APPROVED build is `SHIPPED & APPROVED
MM-DD`, a PENDING_REVIEW build is `SHIPPED, delivered MM-DD, pending review`, and a build missing
from the list is `FAILED_TO_STUMP: backlog`, back in a backlog wave
(`.claude/authors/`, which are lists of numbers and not claims on them). The parser ranks the three as approved, delivered and failed. When
an architecture dies, move it to Earlier architectures in the same edit: a row describing a dead
version misleads the clone screen. task1 to task24 predate the ledger and have cards only.

**Format (parsed).** `clone-check/fingerprint.py` (`parse_ledger`) reads every line starting with
`|` whose first cell starts with a task number and maps the first ten cells by position: task,
status, domain, objective, gap, pattern, mechanism, calibration, artifact, decision. Later cells
are for readers. `guard.py` reads each task's strongest status through it. Keep the task number
first, no pipe inside a cell, and status words from one class only:

| Means | Status words the parser keys on | Rank |
|---|---|---|
| shipped, or approved before the portal | `delivered`, `approved` | 3, the exclusion |
| in build, open, or built and parked | `built ` (with the space), `rebuilt`, `hardened`, `awaiting`, `drawing`, `contested` | 2 |
| abandoned | `retired`, `failed`, `exhausted` | 1, history |

**Reading a row.** Dates are 2026, written MM-DD. Objective: FC forecasting, RCA root cause, AD
anomaly detection, CEX experiment and causal, DESC descriptive, ETL extraction and conformation,
OPP opportunity sizing, DQ data quality monitoring, `-` a retired tag. Domain: PA product
analytics, SCL supply chain, ECON economics, NPO nonprofit, P&E policy and education, DSS
demographic and social science, BOA business operations, AAF accounting and audit, MCR marketing
and consumer research, BIO biology (retired), EPH epidemiology, ENR energy and utilities, BANK
banking and credit risk, QF quantitative finance, ACT actuarial, HDE health economics, PES
physical and environmental sciences, GSD general statistics, HFE human factors, CJ criminology, BCR biomedical
and clinical research.
Gap and pattern are the card's, decisive first; calibration and artifact are on the card (`-`).
"Listed" means on the author's refresh lists of 10-02 or 10-07, which state no status. Rows 41 to
43 follow the folders and cards. The last column records which backlog file a build was listed in
when it shipped, as history and not as a claim: no task belongs to anybody.

## Lessons the outcomes paid for

One line each, with the builds that paid for it. The techniques behind them are in `strategies.md`.

**What strong solvers always do**
- Apply a filed rule to an enumerated population: a computation no rung fixes (36, 37, 49, 61, 97 v3 to v4, 107 v3).
- Rank N filed candidates by a filed rule: the name comes back right however the rule changes (36, 7 solves).
- Align the outcome series to any dated cause; use a chronic condition or a gap with no series to align (36, 83 v1).
- Enumerate a finite menu a back-test selects over and read off the survivor; count the cells first (38, 45, 56 v4, 64 v1, 98 v4).
- Scan a rule recovered from stored labels as one parameter, and test it on every export to find a silent switch (40 cut 2, 98 v4).
- Read every governing document: composing two documents is a reading, not a construction (76 v1).
- Run the textbook default first: deduplication, merged intervals, max flow, pilot decomposition, Kaplan-Meier, explain-away and re-screen (44 v2, 58 v1, 58 v5, 134 v1, 181 v1, 446 v1).
- Reach a hold over flags each explained by a documented structure by meta-inference; a hold counts only when reaching it took work (48 v1).
- Find a population extreme on a rate at a small denominator through its count; sweep one- and two-column aggregates on the series the solver conditions on (48).

**What hands the answer over**
- A decisive rule filed at authority level 1 or narrated in policy wording is executed as written; run the delete-the-clause test (61, 85 v1, 89 v4, 93 v3 cut 1, 103 v1, 111 v1).
- A prompt or ask that requests the back-test, spells out the basis or presupposes the conclusion (49, 56 v4, 61, 77 v1, 98 v2).
- A shipped dimension or screen supporting the naive read is rung 0 with a filename and fails Gate G (42 v1, 74 v1, 85 v2).
- A device whose only defence is silence, once any note names it; sweep the social layer against the device list (65 v2, 76 v1, 78).
- A dictionary that explains public file layouts hands over the plumbing (57 v22).
- A governing identity closing over quantities the pack must ship is an execution exercise (73, 76 v1, 92 v2, 98 v1 to v2, 107 v3).
- A corpus reproducing every closed case without the decisive term is a counter-pin; one returning a single surviving cell is an answer key (45).
- A finer-grain working paper in the pack validated four rules at once (60).
- A natural basis that happens to be right on the graded rows (105 v1, 107 v3); a coarse band that absorbs the decisive move (103 v2).

**What holds**
- A corpus blind by construction (the regime opens after the closed period) confirmed a back-tester in the wrong rule (56 v5); that is the validated decoy the guard now blocks, so a control ties under every basis for a reason true now (`strategies.md` L1). Blindness by tuning or by excluding a class leaves a fingerprint (56 v6, 57 v23, 76 v2).
- A pin the solver reads, executes correctly and is harmed by, such as a billing-grain join that makes a population vanish (56 v6).
- A correct monitor blind by its configured grain, which cannot refer a population split across its cells (48 v2).
- A non-linear move: in a rate-times-exposure world every linear mechanism is absorbed when the rate is re-struck (56 v5, 59).
- Rivals made indeterminate (flat across a threshold band) rather than distant; a set-valued count decorrelated from the obvious count (52).
- A re-root that changes what one unit of the graded sum is; changing which rows enter it does not escape a closed identity (76 v1).
- Grading the quantity, not the name: a which-of-N graded on the payee absorbs the rate error (55 v3, 89 v11).
- Fewer rungs: removing one can buy separation adding one cannot; compute the packing floor and the subset sums first (45, 56 v6).
- Never one live degree of freedom: it cannot hold a ladder (49). A min(capacity, population) makes rungs outside the binding band inert (57 v23).

**The ask layer and the bar**
- A response stumped on the call still clears the bar when the asks are by-products of one forward pass (94 r6, 103 v3, 105 v2, 133 r1, 181 r1 at 52).
- Asks downstream of the construction pay only when nobody lands the call (58 v12.1 moved 38 of 49 there); with one response on it, decoupled asks cost the pair less while ask leakage is under a half (`supplemental-stumping` references A5), so asks stay decoupled.
- An ask whose rule sits beside it, or that is a dictionary line, is a reading exercise (61, 107 v2).
- N asks both top responses fail drag both down: 97/(2 x (87 + N)) loses about three points per five such asks (60 v7).
- Redo the decorrelation count after any prompt repair; the ask mix moves (61).

**Determinism, scope and clones**
- An unforced construction (sample SD over root n) or a literal reading of a filed clause fails the portal's determinism QC (229 v1, 181 r2 on PR-07 4.2).
- A money answer asserts no amount on a half-cent boundary (58 v12). Assert the constraint binds in the data (64 v3, 0.59 of capacity).
- A confound that makes a reported figure misleading forces a refusal and fails Gate G; delete it, do not dress it (44 v2 cut 2).
- The artifacts decide the domain, not the tag: an accounting close is out of scope (56 v5), and so were call-centre operations (89 v7).
- A forward committed figure reads as Forecasting even when nothing is estimated (55).
- A request block can be a template at 1.5 per cent n-gram overlap; compare sentence shapes (63). A hardening loop can land on an earlier driver (130 v2 loop 1 against 73 world 10).
- Generators: `hash()` is salted per process (use blake2b); replacing a seeded draw shifts every later draw; assert key uniqueness across the file and every replayed quantity against the generator (48, 56, 58 v12).

**The WHAT screen** (`stumping` 1.2, paid for by three stage-4 deaths in one wave)
- A decisive step that settles the population, the outcome, the window, the unit or the comparison is a
  dead draw: filing that part makes the computation follow from the clause, not filing it leaves the call
  open. 190 (unobserved members in the population), 192 (the unit of the count, no parameter-free reading)
  and 193 (the comparison population) each passed Gate G and Gate H with every figure reproducing and
  still failed, two on the judge's SEND_BACK and one on measurement before any file was cut.
- Measure the ladder on the record before the note's numbers are written. 192's rung 0 was a clustering
  whose one free parameter ran from 673 to 1 through the golden, and the note's figure for it had never
  been computed.
- The swap test is run on the deciding quantity, not the setting: counting how many periodic processes
  generated a record reads the same with machines on maintenance cycles, so the domain was a backdrop
  however carefully the nouns were chosen (192).

**Pins are paid for** (`supplemental-stumping` Part 5, two builds in one window)
- A pin closes a fork and takes the ask's difficulty with it, because the reading it closed was what the
  device rode on. Pair every pin with a silent primary and measure the cracker sheet, the pair ceiling and
  the mirror leakage on the cut pack afterwards: one build's measured ceiling was 83.5 against a paper 35.1
  with leakage at 100 per cent, discovered only because the author wrote the measurement rather than the
  estimate and let `precut.py` fail.
- A determinism pin written as a handling note reads as a solver checklist. Put each pin in the document
  and the voice the organisation would state it in; the force is in the sentence, not the list.
- Where an ask layer is re-cut, freeze the call: the files it reads byte-identical, every call figure
  unchanged, `freeze.py check --mode rubric` exit 0.

**Convergence is bought from the data, so measure what the data pays** (`determinism-check` §1, task190 architecture 2)
- A method forced by the data's structure is forced only on data with the process's own sampling noise. A
  pack whose amounts come from evenly spaced quantiles of a fixed curve makes every estimator agree: 199
  estimators and 180 fit specifications converged on one answer at a Poisson chi-square per degree of
  freedom of 0.029 bank-wide and 0.004 to 0.026 per segment, tail probabilities below 1e-26, and the judge
  put the honest spread at plus or minus 50 on a 400 figure reported to the nearest 10. The convergence and
  the realism failure were one defect, not two.
- So measure the candidate spread on a realistic pack first, then set the graded granularity from it,
  raising the record's volume where the figure needs finer reporting than the noise supports. Coarsening the
  stated rounding to dodge the measurement is the banned move; stating the figure at the precision the data
  carries is the honest one.
- A convergence claim is asserted over a named candidate set, never stated as agreement: a write-up step
  claiming every window, step width and fit family agreed matched 84 per cent of the judge's own
  1,080-specification grid, and the exceptions named a different largest segment.

**The reader and the judge answer different questions** (`build-pipeline`, stage 4)
- The reader reports what a solver sees and does not recompute; the judge recomputes. Where they disagree on
  what a shipped sentence licenses, the judge settles it: on task190 the reader read a band bound as the
  definition of the graded figure and called a main-call fork, while the judge, cold on the same pack, found
  the estimand filed and the method open with Gates A to E passing. On task194 the reader's narrative said
  regression to the mean and the judge's arithmetic showed the structure sound.
- So relay nothing structural, and re-root nothing, until both reports are in. A reader finding is evidence
  of what a solver sees, which is Gate F, not Gate C. Two builds paid a wasted instruction for this.

**Three ways to buy convergence instead of earning it** (`determinism-check` §1, measured across one wave)
- **The data too smooth.** 199 estimators and 180 fit specifications agreed at a Poisson chi-square per
  degree of freedom of 0.029 because the amounts came from evenly spaced quantiles of a fixed curve
  (task190 architecture 2, pass 1).
- **The candidates inside their own sampling error.** Eight estimators spanned 209 to 236.9 with a sampling
  standard deviation of 28 on the estimate and 24 on the gap between the two families, so the observed
  6-point gap was a typical draw and nothing in honest data separated them (task192 architecture 3). The
  same shape caught task194, whose seven closed placements had a Poisson-scaled chi-square of 0.006 on 6
  degrees of freedom and whose preferred pricing beat its rival by a likelihood ratio of only 1.8 to 1.
- **The agreeing estimator insensitive by construction.** The average of a surplus below a threshold and a
  deficit above it reproduced the golden in 186 of 186 specifications because averaging cancels the fitted
  profile's level, while each side alone spanned a factor of ten (task190 architecture 2, pass 3). The
  architecture was spent at that point: three passes each relocated the artifact rather than removing it.
- So a convergence claim is asserted over the judge's own candidate list, measured on a pack whose noise is
  plausible for the process, and screened for insensitivity. Volume is not the remedy for a specification
  spread: measured at 2x, 4x and 8x with the construction scaled alongside, the relative span fell only
  from 44 per cent to 29, because the residue is bias that scales with the figure rather than noise that
  scales with its square root. What worked was a second independent measurement of the same quantity inside
  the record whose arithmetic refuses the specifications that disagree.

**A graded table can be answered without the analysis** (`supplemental-stumping` 1.1b)
- task190's eight-segment table was 7 of 8 reproducible from a flat 0.82 per cent of each segment's deposit
  count, and 6 of 8 at 0.91 per cent with the headline landing on exactly the golden. Book size alone
  reproduced most of the rubric's widest component. Score the flat-rate, constant and all-nil responses at
  the draw, before any file exists.

**A pin's scope is stated before the sentence is written** (`supplemental-stumping` Part 5)
- Three ordered pins leaked three different things in one session: handling notes that read as a solver
  checklist in a release-note file, pins that closed the readings an ask's devices rode on, and a clause
  added to state a band's role that also said where to build the counterfactual. "State the band's role and
  let the exclusion follow" came back as "that has to come from the segment's trade outside the band", and
  the second half is the method. Name what the sentence may and may not say, and read the wording before the
  pack is cut.

**A gate that reads a number cannot check it** (`supplemental-stumping` Part 7, `determinism-check` §3)
- Two builds in one wave shipped an ask sheet whose pair ceiling was not the pair ceiling, and both
  measured **83.5** once the cracker's path was written as code, against paper figures of 35.1 and 39.4. The
  cause was the same both times and it is not drift: a prose sheet reasons about a solver who reads none of
  the pack's filed sentences, which is the ask's **mirror**, and reports the mirror's misses as the
  cracker's. The cracker is defined by what it does execute, so only code can describe it. `precut.py` now
  fails without a `Measured by:` line naming a script that exists in the build.
- The second shape, same consequence: `abs(v - round(v,u))` is the distance to the printed cell's **centre**
  and the edge is `u/2` minus it, so a build measuring the first reports clearance where it measured
  proximity. One read 0.0129 as its tightest share's clearance when the true distance was 0.0371, and
  picked the wrong tightest cell because the metric, not the data, was wrong. Its judge found the real one
  at 0.0013.
- Both let a gate pass on a figure that was the complement or the mirror of the truth. So the rule is
  general: where a gate reads a number out of prose, the prose names the code that produced it, and the
  measurement runs after every change rather than once at the end, because a determinism repair that closes
  a reading spends the device that rode on it the moment the sentence is filed.
- The disposition that makes this work is a builder's, not a gate's: "a passing gate resting on a number I
  know to be false is worth less than a failing gate telling the truth." Two builds in this wave brought a
  measurement instead of a sheet and both times it changed the decision.

**Unspent, so not mistaken for spent**
- The ledger's old row 41 (displacement under fixed assessment capacity, four outreach arms) was drawn and never built.
